# Skills · H · part 33

885 entries, herdr-new-project to hermes-chatgpt-oauth. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **herdr-new-project**: Provisions a brand new project end-to-end via herdr — creates the directory, a new tab in the calling pane's own herdr workspace, a Claude Code agent started and renamed to match the project (before it's briefed, so session-name-based logging and labels agree from the first prompt), remote control enabled, then handed off to a project-provisioning skill (if you have one) and given the user's brief. Use when - 'new agent session', 'new project session', 'start a new project', 'create a new session for &lt;project&gt;'. ([frostymccool/herdr-new-project](https://github.com/frostymccool/herdr-new-project/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★1)
- **herdr-new-task**: Start a Herdr tab with an agent primed on a task. Use when asked to start a new tab or task for a prompt, issue or thread, optionally naming a workspace, agent, model or effort. ([Geekfish/agent-plugins](https://github.com/Geekfish/agent-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/herdr-extensions/skills/herdr-new-task))
- **herdr-omp**: Use when Stefan asks to start a coding agent/OMP for a branch or project. Defaults to Herdr + normal OMP TUI, reusing an existing branch workspace when possible. ([choephix/my-agents](https://github.com/choephix/my-agents/tree/HEAD/skills/hermes/herdr-omp))
- **herdr-omp-orchestration**: Use when orchestrating issue implementation through Herdr with OMP implementer and reviewer agents; it manages worktrees, review loops, gated merges, and cleanup. ([phntm7/skills](https://github.com/phntm7/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/herdr-omp-orchestration) · ★1)
- **herdr-open**: Offer generated pictures, HTML and the task's relevant links (pull requests, deployed pages, dashboards) at task completion, or open a file or website explicitly requested by the user, on their automatically discovered active device. Markdown is explicit-request only. Works across Claude, Codex, Pi, and OpenCode. ([berlinguyinca/herdr-engineering](https://github.com/berlinguyinca/herdr-engineering/tree/HEAD/skills/herdr-open))
- **herdr-open-in-fork**: In Herdr, open or focus a local Git repository in Fork when asked to open this repo in Fork, bring up Fork, or show this worktree in the Git client. Resolve the requested repo and send its root to Fork. Skip requests to fork a repository, inspect Git state yourself, ordinary repo links, and non-Herdr sessions. ([stevehalliwell/agent-skills](https://github.com/stevehalliwell/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/herdr-open-in-fork))
- **herdr-open-in-vscode**: In Herdr, open local files for the user in VS Code when asked to open that file, show those images, let me read that Markdown, or bring up code for review. Open files in editor tabs with their project context. Skip requests to inspect files yourself, ordinary file links, URLs, and non-Herdr sessions. ([stevehalliwell/agent-skills](https://github.com/stevehalliwell/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/herdr-open-in-vscode))
- **herdr-open-worktree**: Open an existing Git worktree as a Herdr workspace, focus it if already open, and optionally start Claude or Codex there. Use when the user asks to open, move into, or continue work in a worktree through Herdr. Do not use to create or delete worktrees or for general Herdr configuration. ([jmchilton/claude-jmchilton-plugins](https://github.com/jmchilton/claude-jmchilton-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/jmchilton/skills/herdr-open-worktree) · ★1)
- **herdr-opencode-agmsg**: Coordinate user-visible OpenCode workers in Herdr panes with agmsg messaging, an explicit provider order, evidence-based acceptance, and owned-tab cleanup. Use when the user authorizes this Herdr/OpenCode multi-agent workflow or asks to switch providers inside it. Not for ordinary single-agent tasks or for inspecting Herdr without user authorization. ([yuki-inaho/skills](https://github.com/yuki-inaho/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/herdr-opencode-agmsg))
- **herdr-opencode-loop**: herdr 経由で opencode ワーカーを駆動する跨ハーネス実装ループの運用ガイド。issue 委譲 / review 差し戻し / 完了マージ / bundle 運用 / [herdr-relay] リレー / wake・待機で迷ったときに使う。trigger: herdr, opencode loop, worker 委譲, herdr-relay, request-update, closeout ([turtton/evorch](https://github.com/turtton/evorch/tree/HEAD/.opencode/skill/herdr-opencode-loop) · ★1)
